Detailed design for final program:

Proposed structure:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

def _fft_convolve(a,b): full convolution using rfft length n+m-1.
def _score_vec(a): return evaluate? Use local for speed: n=len, s=sum, b=conv, m=max, return 2n*m/s^2.
def _score_list(a): evaluate_sequence.
```
Need be safe with time.

Get parent:
```
def _parent():
  h = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
  if h is not None: arr=np.asarray(h,float).ravel(); arr=np.clip... else ones(320)
```
Ensure length maybe 320.

Normalization: `a = a / a.sum()`, clip 0,1000.

Convolution function:
```
def _conv_full(a):
 n=len(a)
 if n ==0: return array
 if n <= 700: return np.convolve(a,a)
 L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
 F=rfft(a,L); c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
 return c
```
For gradient real full with a and b:
```
def _conv_full2(a,b):
 n=len(a); m=len(b)
 if n*m <= 400000: return np.convolve(a,b)
 L=next_fast_len(n+m-1); return irfft(rfft(a,L)*rfft(b,L), L)[:n+m-1]
```

Soft objective/gradient:

```
def _soft_fg(x, beta, reg=0.0):
 a=_softmax_vec(x)
 q=_conv_full(a)
 # scale for softmax: z=beta*q; z -= q.max()*beta
 z = beta*(q - q.max())
 # exp; if beta large, z min -inf possible; add floor
 z = np.maximum(z, -50)
 e=np.exp(z); sm=e.sum(); m=e/sm
 smax = qmax + math.log(sm)/beta
 val = 2*n*smax
 # gradient a:
 m_rev=m[::-1]
 ga = 2*_conv_full2(a, m_rev)[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
 if reg>0: add second diff penalty gradient
 gx = a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
 return val, gx
```

Optimization call:
```
def _soft_optimize(a0, n, deadline, seed):
 x = np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-300)); x = x - avg + maybe deterministic small noise
 best_a = a0; best_s = _score_vec(a0)
 betas = [1,4,16,64,256,1024,4096]
 for beta in betas:
   if time>deadline break
   res = minimize(fun, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':400,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxcor':20})
   a = softmax(res.x)
   s = _score_vec(a)
   if s<best_s: update
   x = res.x - mean
   # maybe multiple random restarts per beta? We have multiple inits.
 return best_a,best_s
```

LP function min τ:
```
def _lp_min_cross(f, n=None, deadline):
 f normalized sum1.
 # variables: g[0:n], tau[1]
 c = [0...0, 1]
 A_ub rows (2n-1)*n + 1 for each lag: matrix with row f shifted + [-1] for tau? Constraint conv(f,g)_k - tau <=0.
 A_eq [[1...1,0]] b_eq [1]
 bounds [(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)]
 linprog c, A_ub=A, b_ub=zeros, A_eq=..., method='highs'
 return g, tau
```
Sample matrix construction: For each k=0..2n-2, row g coefficients? Constraint ∑_j f[k-j] g_j - τ ≤0, where f index 0..n-1. For row k: A[k, j] = f[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n else 0; A[k,n] = -1. Dense (2n-1)x(n+1). For n=320, 639x321. Build each time. Could be okay. Alternatively use Toeplitz/scipy.linalg.toeplitz? Simpler loop.
Solvemay be memory 639*321*8~1.6MB. HiGHS fine. But many LPs? limit 10-50.

Update from LP direction: `f_new=(1-t)f+t*g`, t adaptive line search to reduce score. If LP not feasible? Min cross always feasible e.g g=f tau=M. Use solution. We can run iterative LP:
```
def _lp_refine(f, deadline):
 f=norm(f)
 best=f
 while time...
   g,tau = _lp_min_cross(best)
   if g None break
   # line search t in [0.1..0.5] maybe choose best t that reduces score, require max conv not too high?
   for t in [0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.75?]: f_new clip norm; s=score; choose if <best
   if no improve: break or random
```
Maybe LP direction might improve. But if g has huge self-conv, line search small. We can evaluate multiple t.

Could use `cvxpy`? More overhead. Linprog enough.

Spectral projection initialization:
```
def _spectral_init(n, iterations=200):
 L = 2*n-1
 a = uniform or random clipped
```

```
def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 300:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
        F=rfft(a,L)
        c=irfft(F*F, L)
        c[:=c[:2*n-1]]
        # due to roundoff negative
        np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
Potential issue: rfft/irfft scaling gives exact convolution? scipy.fft irfft includes 1/n, product FFT unnormalized; yes convolution normal. For real a.
`np.convolve` may have small negative? No nonnegative.

Soft objective:
```
def _soft_fg(x, beta, floor_mix=None):
    a=_softmax01(x,n)
    q=_conv_full(a)
    y = n*q
    ym = y.max()
    z = beta*(y-ym)
    # z min maybe -inf; exp handles
    ez=np.exp(np.maximum(z, -700))
    sumz=ez.sum()
    if sumz <=0: m=np.full(2n-1,1/(2n-1)) else m=ez/sumz
    H = ym + math.log(sumz)/beta if beta>0 else ym
    val=2*H
    # m_rev = m[::-1]
    V = _conv_valid(a,m)
    qgrad = 2*V
    grad_a = 2*n*qgrad  # for score
    grad_x = a*(grad_a - np.dot(a,grad_a))
    return val, grad_x
```
Check for beta=0? We won't use 0. For large beta, z min -700 may be clipped but exp underflows; okay. `log(sumz)` if sumz 0? Use uniform.

```
def _lp_min_cross(f):
 n=f.size
 A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n+1))
 # columns for g: for lag k, A[k, j] = f[k-j]
 for j in range(n):
    idx = np.arange(j, j+2*n-1? no, k from j to j+n-1 where k-j in 0..n-1). Simpler:
    # f index d = k-j. d range 0..n-1 => k=j+d. d 0..n-1.
    A[j:j+n, j] = f
 A[:, n] = -1
 c = np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
 A_eq=np.array([[np.ones(n),0.0]]); b_eq=[1.0]
 b_ub=np.zeros(2*n-1)
 res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=[(0,None)]*n+[(0,None)], method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
 if res.status==0:
  g=res.x[:n]; tau=res.x[n]
 else None.
```

```
   # If tau >= score/(2n)???

      cand=(1-t)*f0 + t*g; clip norm; s=score(cand)
      if s<best_s: choose
   if best_t==0: stall++; if stall>3 break; f0 = np.clip(f0+normal small)*norm
   else f0=cand; best=cand; best_s=s; stall=0
```

```
def _polish(a, deadline):
 a=norm(a); best=score(a); noimp=0; rng
 while time<deadline-10 and noimp<8:
   improved=False
   order=rng.permutation(n)
   for i in order:
     if time>deadline-10: break
     orig=a[i]
     # choose multipliers based on current derivative? Use (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5,2.0,0.0)
     best_ml=1; best_sc=best
     for ml in muls:
       if ml==0: new=a; new[i]=0
       else: new=a.copy(); new[i]=orig*ml; if new.sum()>1e-12: new/=new.sum() else continue
       sc=score(new)
       if sc<best_sc-1e-13: best_sc=sc; best_ml=ml
     if best_ml !=1:
       a = ... normalized; best=best_sc; improved=True
   # two-coordinate maybe limited
   if not improved: noimp +=1 else noimp=0
 return a,best
```

Let's implement a simple DFT amplitude shaping as initializer:
We want q=conv(a,a) bounded. In zero-padded DFT length L=2n-1, Q=FFT(a padded)^2. If we set FFT(a) magnitude to a target T and phase from current, then a new = ifft(magnitude*phase)[:n]. If Q is flat, q is delta at lag 0 (circular) = ∑a_i^2 at m=0. Not our objective? We want q flat across lags, which corresponds to Q having only Fourier mode? If q flat M, its DFT has low frequency nonzero, high freq zero. A² should have only DC term? That would mean |A|² constant? Wait DFT of q flat is DC large, other zero. Implementation of `_soft_parts(x,beta)`:
```
def _soft_parts(x,beta):
    x = x - x.mean()
    x = x - x.max()
    x = np.clip(x, -500.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
    a = e/s
    q = _conv_full(a)
    y = n * q
    ym = float(y.max())
    z = beta * (y - ym)
    ez = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
    ssum = ez.sum()
    if not finite or ssum<=0:
       m = np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
       H = ym
    else:
       m = ez/ssum
       H = ym + math.log(ssum)/beta
    V = _conv_valid(a,m)
    grad_a = 4.0 * n * V  # as derived? Wait grad_a for score =4nV. V = sum m a.
    # Check: if active single, V=a_{K-l}; grad=4n a. Correct.
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - float(np.dot(a, grad_a)))
    val = 2.0 * H
    return val, grad_x
```

```
def _run_minimize(fun_factory, x, limit, options):
  state={'x': x.copy()}
  def fun(xx):
     if time.monotonic() > limit: raise _OptStop
     val, grad = fun_factory(xx)
     state['x'] = xx.copy()
     # validate
     if not finite val or all grad nonfinite: return val+...? Raise? Maybe return 1e100 and zero grad to terminate? Could cause. Better raise _OptStop? If numerical bad, return 1e100, grad zero.
     return val, grad
  def cb(xk):
     if time.monotonic() > limit: raise _OptStop
     state['x']=xk.copy()
  try: res = minimize(...)
  except _OptStop: res = None
  return state['x'], res
```

Detailed:
```
def _polish(a, stop):
 a = np.array(a,float).ravel()
 a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
 s=a.sum()
 if s<=1e-12: return ones
 a = a/s  # start normalize
 best_a=a.copy(); best_s=score(a)
 rng=default_rng(12345)
 noimp=0
 muls=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.7,1.3,1.5,2.0)
 while time<stop-5 and noimp<8:
  improved=False
  order=rng.permutation(len(a))
  # maybe skip very small entries? no
  for i in order:
    if time>stop-5: break
    orig=a[i]
    if orig < 0: orig=0
    best_ml=1.0; best_sc=best_s
    for ml in muls:
       if time>stop-5: break
       if ml == 0.0:
          val=0.0
       elif orig > 1e-15:
          val=orig*ml
       else:
          val=1e-5  # try introduce
       a[i]=val
       sc=score_vec(a)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_sc=sc; best_ml=ml
    if best_ml != 1.0:
       # if val uninitialized? recompute val
       if best_ml==0: val=0
       elif orig>1e-15: val=orig*best_ml
       else: val=1e-5
       a[i]=val
       best_s=best_sc; best_a=a.copy(); improved=True
    else:
       a[i]=orig
  # two-coordinate
  tries=40 if len(a)>200 else 80
  for _ in range(tries):
    if time>stop-5: break
    i,j=rng.integers(0,n),rng.integers(0,n)
    if i==j: continue
    oi=a[i]; oj=a[j]
    local_best=best_s; local_i_ml=1; local_j_ml=1
    for mi in (0.5,1.5,0.0):
      for mj in (0.5,1.5,0.0):
        if mi==0: vi=0
        elif oi>1e-15: vi=oi*mi
        else: vi=1e-5
        ...
        a[i]=vi; a[j]=vj; sc=score_vec(a)
        if sc<local_best-1e-13: update
    if local_best<best_s-1e-13: accept else restore
  if not improved: noimp +=1
  else: noimp=0
 # normalize best
 best_a=best_a/best_a.sum()
 return best_a, score_vec(best_a)
```
But in coordinate loop, after trying multipliers, we restore only if no acceptance; however during trying, `a[i]` changes. If accept, set to val and continue; next coordinate uses updated. Good. Need compute `val` on accept. If `best_ml==1`, restore orig. `best_ml` float.

```
def _make_inits(n, parent, seed=0, num=5):
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)

 # parent interp
 def interp_parent():
   if parent.size==n:
      p=parent.copy()
   else:
      p=np.interp(np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n), np.linspace(0.0,1.0,parent.size), parent)
   p=np.clip(p,0,None)
   if p.max()<1e-8 or p.sum()<1e-8: p=np.ones(n)
   p += 1e-6
   return p/p.sum()
 inits.append(interp_parent())
 inits.append(_noise(init=...))
 # uniform with noise
 uni=np.ones(n)+rng.normal(0,0.05,n); inits.append(normalize max(0))
 # Fourier random for _ in range(max(0,num-3))
 for trial in range(...):
   x=np.zeros(n)
   for freq in range(1,5):
      if trial%2: use sin/cos
      amp=rng.uniform(0.3,1.8)
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*pi)
      x += amp*np.cos(2*pi*freq*i/n+ph)
   x += rng.normal(0,0.1,n)
   a=np.exp(0.7*x); normalize

 # endpoint-heavy
 for alpha in [0.6,-0.6]: a=1+alpha*np.cos(2π i/n); clip, norm
 return inits
```

```
def _normalize(a):

 if a.size==0: a=np.ones(1)

 a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
 s=a.sum()
 if s <=1e-15: return np.full(a.size,1.0/max(1,a.size))
 return a/s
```

```
def _conv_valid(a,m):
```

`_run_minimize(fun, x0, limit, n)`:
```
def _run_minimize(fun, x0, limit, n):
 x0=np.asarray(x0,float).ravel()
 if x0.size != n: adjust
 x0=np.where(np.isfinite(x0),x0,0.0)
 x0-=np.mean(x0); x0=np.clip(x0,-400,400)
 st={'x':x0.copy()}
 maxiter=int(max(20, min(300, 90000//max(1,n))))
 def cb(xk):
    if time.monotonic() > limit:
       raise _OptStop
    st['x']=np.asarray(xk,float).copy()
 def funnel(xx):
    if time.monotonic() > limit:
       raise _OptStop
    val, grad = fun(xx)
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100
    grad=np.asarray(grad,float).ravel()
    if grad.size != n: grad=np.zeros(n)
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    gn=np.linalg.norm(grad)
    if gn > 1e50: grad *= 1e50/gn
    elif gn ==0: pass
    st['x']=np.asarray(xx,float).copy()
    return float(val), grad
 try:
    res=minimize(funnel, st['x'], jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', callback=cb,
                 options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-9,'maxcor':15,'maxls':30})
 except _OptStop:
    res=None
 except Exception:
    res=None
 if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
    x=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
    if x.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        st['x']=x
 x=st['x']; x-=np.mean(x); x=np.clip(x,-400,400)
 return x
```
Potential: `funnel` updates st['x'] even if val large; okay. If time limit exceeded after computing, no update? It checks at top only; during compute no. Fine.

`_soft_fg(xx,beta,n)`:

```
def _soft_fg(xx,beta,n):
 x=np.asarray(xx,float).ravel(); x=np.where(finite,0,x); x=x-x.mean(); x=x-x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-500,0)
 e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum(); if bad: val=2.0; grad zeros? better uniform.
 a=e/s
 q=_conv_full(a); y=n*q; ym=float(np.max(y)) if y.size else 0
 z=beta*(y-ym)
 ez=np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
 ss=ez.sum()
 if ss<=0 or not finite:
   m=np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1)); H=ym
 else:
   m=ez/ss; H=ym + math.log(ss)/beta
 val=2.0*H
 V=_conv_valid(a,m)
 grad_a=4.0*n*V
 grad_x=a*(grad_a - float(np.dot(a,grad_a)))
 return val, grad_x
```

`_lp_min_cross` needs `a` normalized. Build A using loop. Might take time; check time before.
```
def _lp_min_cross(a):
 n=a.size; K=2*n-1
 A=np.zeros((K,n+1),float); A[:,n]=-1
 for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=a
 c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
 b_ub=np.zeros(K)
 A_eq=np.array([[np.ones(n),0.0]])
 b_eq=np.array([1.0])
 bounds=[(0.0,None)]*(n+1)
 res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
 if res.status==0:
   g=res.x[:n]
   return g
 return None
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


class _OptStop(Exception):
    pass


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    return a


def _get_parent():
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.size > 0 and float(np.sum(p)) > 1e-12:
                return p
        except Exception:
            pass
    return np.ones(320, dtype=np.float64)


def _resample(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == m:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def _conv_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a * a
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a, mode="full")
    else:
        L = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        F = rfft(a, L)
        c = irfft(F * F, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_vec(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    c = _conv_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]))
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _conv_valid(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = np.asarray(m, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if m.size < need:
        m = np.pad(m, (0, need - m.size), mode="constant")
    elif m.size > need:
        m = m[:need]
    mr = m[::-1]
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, mr, mode="full")
    else:
        L = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
        c = irfft(rfft(a, L) * rfft(mr, L), L)[: 3 * n - 2]
    out = np.asarray(c[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1][::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def _softmax_vec(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        if x.size > n:
            x = x[:n]
        else:
            x = np.pad(x, (0, n - x.size), mode="constant")
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _log_init(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    x = np.log(a)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    return np.clip(x, -400.0, 400.0)


def _soft_fg(xx, beta, n):
    x = np.asarray(xx, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        if x.size > n:
            x = x[:n]
        else:
            x = np.pad(x, (0, n - x.size), mode="constant")
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(es):
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        q = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / (2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = e / es
        q = _conv_full(a)

    y = float(n) * q
    ym = float(np.max(y)) if y.size else 0.0
    z = beta * (y - ym)
    ez = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
    ss = float(ez.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ss):
        m = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / max(1, 2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        h = ym
    else:
        m = ez / ss
        h = ym + math.log(ss) / beta

    val = 2.0 * h
    V = _conv_valid(a, m)
    ga = 4.0 * float(n) * V
    gx = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return float(val), gx


def _weighted_weights_from_q(a, beta, mix):
    q = _conv_full(a)
    y = float(a.size) * q
    K = q.size
    ym = float(np.max(y)) if K else 0.0
    z = beta * (y - ym)
    ez = np.exp(np.maximum(z, -745.0))
    ss = float(ez.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ss):
        m = np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        m = ez / ss
    if mix > 0.0:
        m = (1.0 - mix) * m + (mix / K)
    s = float(m.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(K, 1.0 / K, dtype=np.float64)
    return m / s


def _weighted_fg(xx, w, n):
    a = _softmax_vec(xx, n)
    q = _conv_full(a)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != q.size:
        if w.size > q.size:
            w = w[: q.size]
        else:
            w = np.pad(w, (0, q.size - w.size), mode="constant")
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.full(q.size, 1.0 / q.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.dot(w, q))
    V = _conv_valid(a, w)
    ga = 4.0 * float(n) * V
    gx = a * (ga - float(np.dot(a, ga)))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.where(np.isfinite(gx), gx, 0.0)
    return float(val), gx


def _run_minimize(fun, x0, limit, n):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x0.size != n:
        if x0.size > n:
            x0 = x0[:n]
        else:
            x0 = np.pad(x0, (0, n - x0.size), mode="constant")
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)
    x0 = x0 - float(np.mean(x0))
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -400.0, 400.0)

    st = {"x": x0.copy()}

    def funnel(xx):
        if time.monotonic() > limit:
            raise _OptStop
        val, grad = fun(xx)
        grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if grad.size != n:
            grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            val = 1e100
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gn > 1e50:
            grad *= (1e50 / gn)
        st["x"] = np.asarray(xx, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        st["x"] = np.where(np.isfinite(st["x"]), st["x"], 0.0)
        return float(val), grad

    def callback(xk):
        if time.monotonic() > limit:
            raise _OptStop
        xk = np.asarray(xk, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if xk.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(xk)):
            st["x"] = xk.copy()

    maxiter = int(max(20, min(300, 90000 // max(1, n))))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            funnel,
            st["x"],
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            callback=callback,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "ftol": 1e-13,
                "gtol": 1e-9,
                "maxcor": 15,
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            rr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if rr.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(rr)):
                st["x"] = rr
    except _OptStop:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    x = np.asarray(st["x"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    return np.clip(x, -400.0, 400.0)


def _soft_optimize(a0, n, stop, betas=None):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(n)
    a = a0.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score_vec(a)

    if betas is None:
        betas = [1, 8, 64, 512, 4096]
    betas = [float(b) for b in betas]

    x = _log_init(a)
    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 3.0:
            break
        remaining = max(1, len(betas) - idx)
        beta_stop = min(
            stop - 2.0,
            max(time.monotonic() + 4.0, time.monotonic() + (stop - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining)),
        )
        try:
            x = _run_minimize(lambda xx, b=beta: _soft_fg(xx, b, n), x, beta_stop, n)
        except Exception:
            break
        a = _softmax_vec(x, n)
        s = _score_vec(a)
        if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_s - 1e-13:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a.copy()
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 5.0:
            break

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    return best_a, best_s


def _weighted_optimize(a0, n, stop, betas=None):
    a0 = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(n)
    a = a0.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score_vec(a)

    if betas is None:
        betas = [8, 32, 128, 1024]
    betas = [float(b) for b in betas]

    x = _log_init(a)
    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
            break
        remaining = max(1, len(betas) - idx + 1)
        beta_stop = min(
            stop - 3.0,
            max(time.monotonic() + 4.0, time.monotonic() + (stop - time.monotonic()) / float(remaining)),
        )

        mix = 0.35 if beta < 32.0 else (0.15 if beta < 256.0 else 0.05)
        w = _weighted_weights_from_q(a, beta, mix)
        try:
            x = _run_minimize(lambda xx, ww=w: _weighted_fg(xx, ww, n), x, beta_stop, n)
        except Exception:
            break

        a = _softmax_vec(x, n)
        s = _score_vec(a)
        if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_s - 1e-13:
            best_s = s
            best_a = a.copy()

        if time.monotonic() > stop - 25.0:
            break

    if time.monotonic() < stop - 12.0:
        try:
            q = _conv_full(a)
            M = float(q.max()) if q.size else 0.0
            tol = max(1e-10, 1e-3 * M)
            active = q > (M - tol)
            if 0 < int(active.sum()) < 0.85 * q.size:
                w = active.astype(np.float64) / float(active.sum())
            else:
                w = _weighted_weights_from_q(a, 128.0, 0.05)
            beta_stop = stop - 4.0
            x = _run_minimize(lambda xx, ww=w: _weighted_fg(xx, ww, n), x, beta_stop, n)
            a = _softmax_vec(x, n)
            s = _score_vec(a)
            if math.isfinite(s) and s < best_s - 1e-13:
                best_s = s
                best_a = a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    return best_a, best_s


def _lp_min_cross(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    K = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((K, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:, n] = -1.0
    for j in range(n):
        A[j : j + n, j] = a
    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0
    A_eq = np.array([[np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0]])
    b_eq = np.array([1.0])
    b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or getattr(res, "status", 1) != 0:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    if float(g.sum()) < 1e-14:
        return None
    return g


def _lp_refine(a0, stop, max_iters=5):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score_vec(a)
    if n <= 0 or n > 512:
        return best_a, best_s

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 45.0:
            break
        g = _lp_min_cross(a)
        if g is None:
            break
        g = _normalize(g)

        accepted = False
        best_c = None
        for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
            sc = _score_vec(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = sc
                best_c = cand
                accepted = True
        if not accepted or best_c is None:
            break
        best_a = _normalize(best_c)
        best_s = _score_vec(best_a)
        a = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_s


def _polish(a0, stop):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or time.monotonic() > stop - 3.0:
        return a, _score_vec(a)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score_vec(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    if n <= 420:
        muls = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0)
        max_sweeps = 8
        subset_size = n
    else:
        muls = (0.0, 0.5, 1.5, 2.0)
        max_sweeps = 4
        subset_size = max(60, n // 3)

    no_imp = 0
    for _ in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
            break
        improved = False

        if subset_size >= n:
            order = rng.permutation(n)
        else:
            order = rng.choice(n, size=subset_size, replace=False)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
                break
            i = int(i)
            orig = float(a[i])
            best_ml = 1.0
            best_sc = best_s
            for ml in muls:
                if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
                    break
                if ml == 0.0:
                    val = 0.0
                elif orig > 1e-15:
                    val = orig * ml
                else:
                    val = 1e-6
                a[i] = val
                if val < 0.0:
                    a[i] = 0.0
                sc = _score_vec(a)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_ml = float(ml)

            if best_ml != 1.0:
                if best_ml == 0.0:
                    val = 0.0
                elif orig > 1e-15:
                    val = orig * best_ml
                else:
                    val = 1e-6
                a[i] = max(0.0, val)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-15:
                    a /= s
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_s = best_sc
                improved = True
            else:
                a[i] = orig

        two_tries = 40 if n <= 500 else 20
        for _t in range(two_tries):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
                break
            i, j = int(rng.integers(0, n)), int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            oi, oj = float(a[i]), float(a[j])
            li, lj, ls = 1.0, 1.0, best_s
            for mi in (0.0, 0.5, 1.5):
                for mj in (0.0, 0.5, 1.5):
                    if time.monotonic() > stop - 4.0:
                        break
                    vi = 0.0 if mi == 0.0 else (oi * mi if oi > 1e-15 else 1e-6)
                    vj = 0.0 if mj == 0.0 else (oj * mj if oj > 1e-15 else 1e-6)
                    a[i], a[j] = max(0.0, vi), max(0.0, vj)
                    sc = _score_vec(a)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < ls - 1e-13:
                        ls, li, lj = sc, float(mi), float(mj)

            if ls < best_s - 1e-13:
                if li == 0.0:
                    vi = 0.0
                elif oi > 1e-15:
                    vi = oi * li
                else:
                    vi = 1e-6
                if lj == 0.0:
                    vj = 0.0
                elif oj > 1e-15:
                    vj = oj * lj
                else:
                    vj = 1e-6
                a[i], a[j] = max(0.0, vi), max(0.0, vj)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-15:
                    a /= s
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_s = ls
                improved = True
            else:
                a[i], a[j] = oi, oj

        if not improved:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp >= 8:
                break
        else:
            no_imp = 0

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)
    return best_a, _score_vec(best_a)


def _make_inits(n, parent, seed=0, num=5):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return [np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = []

    def add(arr):
        if len(out) >= num:
            return
        try:
            b = _normalize(arr)
            if b.size == n and math.isfinite(_score_vec(b)):
                out.append(b)
        except Exception:
            pass

    p = _resample(parent, n)
    add(p + 1e-6 + 1e-3 * max(1.0, float(np.mean(p))) * rng.standard_normal(n))
    if n > 1:
        add(0.5 * (p + p[::-1]) + 1e-6 + 1e-3 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    add(np.ones(n) + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n))

    for trial in range(2):
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for fr in range(1, 5):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.8))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            x += amp * np.cos((2.0 * math.pi * fr * i) / float(n) + ph)
            if trial == 1:
                ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
                x += 0.5 * amp * np.sin((2.0 * math.pi * fr * i) / float(n) + ph2)
        x += 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        a = np.exp(np.clip(x, -5.0, 5.0))
        add(a)

    if n > 1:
        add(1.0 + 0.65 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n)) + 1e-4 * rng.standard_normal(n))
        add(1.0 - 0.55 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n)) + 1e-4 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    else:
        add(np.ones(n))

    if not out:
        out.append(_normalize(np.ones(n)))
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    head = min(20.0, max(2.0, 0.05 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - head
    if deadline <= t0 + 2.0:
        deadline = t0 + budget - max(1.0, 0.1 * budget)

    seed_i = int(seed) if seed is not None else 42

    parent = _get_parent()
    fallback = _normalize(parent)
    if fallback.size == 0 or not math.isfinite(_score_vec(fallback)):
        fallback = _normalize(np.ones(320))

    best_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_exact = _exact_score(best_seq)
    if not math.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_exact = _score_vec(best_seq)
    best_local = _score_vec(best_seq)

    def _maybe_update(a):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_exact, best_local
        try:
            b = _normalize(a)
        except Exception:
            return
        if b.size == 0:
            return
        sc_local = _score_vec(b)
        if not math.isfinite(sc_local):
            return
        try:
            sc_exact = _exact_score(b)
        except Exception:
            sc_exact = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < best_exact - 1e-13:
            best_seq = b.copy()
            best_exact = float(sc_exact)
            best_local = float(sc_local)
        elif math.isfinite(sc_local) and sc_local < best_local - 1e-12:
            best_local = float(sc_local)

    n_p = int(fallback.size)

    # Primary length: parent length, with several starts and local refinement.
    if best_exact > 1.51:
        num_primary = 5
    elif best_exact > 1.5025:
        num_primary = 4
    else:
        num_primary = 3

    phase1_end = t0 + budget * 0.50
    inits_p = _make_inits(n_p, parent, seed_i, num_primary)
    for idx, init in enumerate(inits_p):
        if time.monotonic() > phase1_end - 25.0:
            break
        stop = min(
            phase1_end - 5.0,
            t0 + (phase1_end - t0) * (idx + 1) / max(1.0, float(len(inits_p))),
        )
        try:
            a_br, _ = _soft_optimize(init, n_p, stop, betas=[1, 8, 64, 512, 4096])
        except Exception:
            a_br = init
        _maybe_update(a_br)

    if time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 40.0:
        try:
            a_w, _ = _weighted_optimize(best_seq, len(best_seq), phase1_end - 15.0, betas=[16, 64, 256, 1024])
        except Exception:
            a_w = best_seq
        _maybe_update(a_w)

    if len(best_seq) <= 512 and time.monotonic() < phase1_end - 35.0:
        try:
            a_lp, _ = _lp_refine(best_seq, phase1_end - 10.0, max_iters=3)
        except Exception:
            a_lp = best_seq
        _maybe_update(a_lp)

    # Other lengths, if available time remains.
    phase2_end = t0 + budget * 0.72
    if time.monotonic() < phase2_end - 30.0:
        alt = []
        base_n = n_p
        candidates = [
            max(96, base_n // 2),
            min(1024, int(1.5 * base_n)),
            min(1024, 2 * base_n),
            256,
            320,
            384,
            512,
            640,
        ]
        for L in candidates:
            if int(L) != n_p and 96 <= int(L) <= 1024 and int(L) not in alt:
                alt.append(int(L))
        alt = sorted(alt)[:3]

        if alt:
            time_per = max(30.0, (phase2_end - time.monotonic()) / float(len(alt)))
            for L in alt:
                if time.monotonic() > phase2_end - 20.0:
                    break
                stop = min(phase2_end - 5.0, time.monotonic() + time_per * 0.95)
                if stop <= time.monotonic() + 1.0:
                    break
                try:
                    inits_L = _make_inits(L, best_seq, seed_i + 10000 + L, 1)
                    a_br, _ = _soft_optimize(inits_L[0], L, stop, betas=[8, 64, 512])
                except Exception:
                    a_br = _resample(best_seq, L)
                _maybe_update(a_br)

    # Final refinement on the current best.
    refine_end = deadline - 10.0
    if time.monotonic() < refine_end - 80.0:
        Lb = int(len(best_seq))
        stop_w = min(refine_end - 40.0, time.monotonic() + max(30.0, (refine_end - time.monotonic()) / 2.0))
        try:
            a_w, _ = _weighted_optimize(best_seq, Lb, stop_w, betas=[32, 128, 512])
        except Exception:
            a_w = best_seq
        _maybe_update(a_w)

    if len(best_seq) <= 512 and time.monotonic() < refine_end - 60.0:
        stop_lp = min(refine_end - 40.0, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, (refine_end - time.monotonic()) / 2.0))
        try:
            a_lp, _ = _lp_refine(best_seq, stop_lp, max_iters=3)
        except Exception:
            a_lp = best_seq
        _maybe_update(a_lp)

    if time.monotonic() < refine_end - 8.0:
        try:
            a_pol, _ = _polish(best_seq, refine_end - 4.0)
        except Exception:
            a_pol = best_seq
        _maybe_update(a_pol)

    out = _normalize(best_seq)
    if out.size == 0 or float(out.sum()) < 0.01:
        out = fallback
    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        out = out / s

    lst = [float(x) for x in out]
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        sc = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        lst = [float(x) for x in fallback]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            sc = math.inf
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            return [1.0]

    return lst
```
